id="main-content" tabindex="-1"></span></span><div class="topicMain__container"><div dir="ltr" class="sc-690ca30c-0 cmrsaE"><div class="pageTitle__container"><div class="pageTitle__content"><div class="pageTitle__title"><h1>Report a spill</h1></div></div></div></div><span class="last_Updated_Text"><img src="/icons/last-updated-star.svg" alt=""/> <!-- -->Last updated on August 2, 2024</span><div class="topicContent__main"><div id="cmf-ui-alerts" class="sc-5dc6d7e7-0 cmWUnZ"><div class="alerts"><div class="sc-3d886b8f-0 eQSpUt alert alert-information" role="alert"><div class="alert-icon"><img src="/icons/info-circle.svg" alt="information"/></div><div class="alert-message"><p><span>A new spill notification service using Alertable is currently being rolled out to all First Nations across British Columbia. First Nations can reach out to <a href="mailto:spillresponse@gov.bc.ca" target="_self">spillresponse@gov.bc.ca</a> to express interest.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div id="introduction" class="styled-h2"><p><img alt="Report a Spill at 1-800-663-3456" src="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/environment/air-land-water/spills-and-environmental-emergencies/images/report_a_spill_graphic-forweb.jpg" style="display:block; float:initial; margin:auto" title="Report a Spill at 1-800-663-3456" /></p>

<hr /></div><div id="body" class="styled-h2"><p class="bc_paragraph"><strong>When a spill or the risk of a spill occurs, report it immediately by calling 1-800-663-3456. </strong></p>

<p class="bc_paragraph">This is known as the Initial Report or Dangerous Goods Incident Report (DGIR).</p>

<p>The Initial Report must be completed by the responsible person (spiller)&nbsp;if:</p>

<ul>
	<li>The quantity of the spill&nbsp;is equal to or greater than the quantity outlined in&nbsp;<a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#Schedule" target="_self">the schedule of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a> for a specific substance</li>
	<li>The spill has impacted, or might impact, a body of water</li>
</ul>

<p class="bc_paragraph">Additional information on spill reporting requirements is available in the&nbsp;<a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/crbc/crbc/187_2017" target="_blank">Spill Reporting Regulation (PDF, 79KB)</a>&nbsp;of the&nbsp;<a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/03053_00" target="_self">Environmental Management Act</a>.</p>

<h2 class="bc_h2">On this page</h2>

<ul>
	<li><a href="#sheens" target="_self">Water sheens</a></li>
	<li><a href="#initial" target="_self">Initial Report</a></li>
	<li><a href="#update" target="_self">Update to Minister Report</a></li>
	<li><a href="#end" target="_self">End-of-Spill Report</a></li>
	<li><a href="#lesson" target="_self">Lessons-Learned Report</a></li>
</ul>

<hr />
<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="sheens" name="sheens"></a>Water sheens</h3>

<p><span>Sheens can be caused by petroleum products in the water, or they can occur naturally.</span></p>

<p>Before you report a spill, learn more about water sheens:</p>

<ul>
	<li>Are&nbsp;you looking at a sheen caused by a spill?</li>
	<li>Is your sheen naturally occurring?</li>
</ul>

<p><span>Here&#39;s&nbsp;how to tell the difference between naturally occurring sheens and those&nbsp;caused by pollution:</span></p>

<ul>
	<li><a href="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/environment/air-land-water/spills-and-environmental-emergencies/docs/materials/watersheen_facts__identifying_sheen.pdf" target="_blank"><span>Water Sheen Fact Sheet (PDF, 1.5MB)</span></a></li>
</ul>

<hr />
<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="initial" name="initial"></a>Initial Report</h3>

<p>When reporting a spill, the following information must be provided to the dispatcher:</p>

<ul>
	<li>The contact information for the individual making the report,&nbsp;the responsible person in relation to the spill and the owner of the substance spilled</li>
	<li>The date and time of the spill</li>
	<li>The location of the spill site</li>
	<li>A description of the spill site and the surrounding area</li>
	<li>A description of the source of the spill</li>
	<li>The type and quantity of the substance spilled</li>
	<li>A description of the circumstances, cause and adverse effects of the spill</li>
	<li>Details of any action taken or proposed to comply with <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/03053_08#section91.2" target="_self">Section 91.2 (2)</a> of the <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/03053_00" target="_self">Environmental Management Act</a>
	<ul>
		<li>Review the <a href="https://www2.gov.bc.ca/assets/gov/environment/air-land-water/spills-and-environmental-emergencies/docs/materials/fact_sheet_responsible_person.pdf" target="_blank">Responsible Persons Spill Response Fact Sheet (PDF, 232KB)</a></li>
	</ul>
	</li>
	<li>Names of any&nbsp;provincial, federal, local and/or First Nations government agencies at the spill site</li>
	<li>The names of any other persons or government agencies advised about the spill</li>
</ul>

<hr />
<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="update" name="update"></a>Update to Minister Report</h3>

<p>As of October 30, 2018, the <a href="#template" target="_self">Update to Minister Report</a> is required and must be completed by the responsible person in relation to a spill with the information outlined in <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section5" target="_self">Section 5 of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a>.</p>

<p>This report is required if any of the following 3 conditions are present:</p>

<ul>
	<li>As soon as possible at the&nbsp;request of the minister</li>
	<li>At least once every 30 days after the date that the spill began until such time that the End-of-Spill Report is required</li>
	<li>At any time the responsible person has reason to believe that information previously reported as part of the Initial Report was or has become inaccurate</li>
</ul>

<hr />
<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="end" name="end"></a>End of Spill Report</h3>

<p>As of October 30, 2018, the <a href="#template" target="_self">End-of-Spill Report</a> is required and must be completed by the responsible person in relation to a spill with the information outlined in <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section6" target="_self">Section 6 of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a>.</p>

<p>This report is required&nbsp;if:</p>

<ul>
	<li>The quantity spilled, or likely to spill, is equal to or greater than the quantity outlined in the Spill Reporting Regulation&nbsp;for a specific substance</li>
	<li>The spill impacted, or was likely to impact, a body of water</li>
</ul>

<p>If a spill meets the requirements for this report, the responsible person must complete the Update to Minister/End-of-Spill Report using the template provided on this page.</p>

<p>Once completed, <a href="mailto:SpillReports@gov.bc.ca" target="_self">submit the report by email</a> stating the DGIR number with &lsquo;End-of-Spill Report&rsquo; in the subject line.</p>

<p>If required, this report must be submitted within 30 days of the emergency response completion date of the spill as defined in <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section8" target="_self">Section 8 of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a>.</p>

<hr />
<h3 class="bc_h3"><a id="lesson" name="lesson"></a>Lessons-learned Report</h3>

<p>A director may order a responsible person to submit a Lessons-Learned Report in relation to a spill incident as outlined in <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section7" target="_self">Section 7 of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a>. This should occur within 6 months of the emergency response completion date outlined in <a href="http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section8" target="_self">Section 8 of the Spill Reporting Regulation</a>.</p>

<p>If someone is ordered to submit a Lessons-Learned Report, they&#39;ll receive the order via registered mail with instructions on how to complete and submit the report.</p></div><div class="topicPageNav__container nav_Tab"><div id="cmf-ui-page-navigation" class="sc-15ed282c-0 lfWRri"><button aria-label="Open More topics menu"><h2>More topics</h2><span style="box-sizing:border-box;display:inline-block;overflow:hidden;width:initial;height:initial;background:none;opacity:1;border:0;margin:0;padding:0;position:relative;max-width:100%"><span style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;width:initial;height:initial;background:none;opacity:1;border:0;margin:0;padding:0;max-width:100%"><img style="display:block;max-width:100%;width:initial;height:initial;background:none;opacity:1;border:0;margin:0;padding:0" alt="" aria-hidden="true" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3csvg%20xmlns=%27http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%27%20version=%271.1%27%20width=%2716%27%20height=%2716%27/%3e"/></span><img alt="" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7" decoding="async" data-nimg="intrinsic" style="position:absolute;top:0;left:0;bottom:0;right:0;box-sizing:border-box;padding:0;border:none;margin:auto;display:block;width:0;height:0;min-width:100%;max-width:100%;min-height:100%;max-height:100%"/><noscript><img alt="" srcSet="/icons/list.svg 1x, /icons/list.svg 2x" src="/icons/list.svg" decoding="async" data-nimg="intrinsic" style="position:absolute;top:0;left:0;bottom:0;right:0;box-sizing:border-box;padding:0;border:none;margin:auto;display:block;width:0;height:0;min-width:100%;max-width:100%;min-height:100%;max-height:100%" loading="lazy"/></noscript></span></button></div></div></div><div id="cmf-ui-supplementary-content" class="sc-482527c5-0 hgXFlr"><div dir="ltr"><div class="sc-2b15949a-0 drDTsU"><h2 class="banner">Report template</h2><div class="relatedLinks__content"><div class="relatedLinks__intro"><ul>

spill\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;The location of the spill site\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;A description of the spill site and the surrounding area\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;A description of the source of the spill\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;The type and quantity of the substance spilled\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;A description of the circumstances, cause and adverse effects of the spill\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;Details of any action taken or proposed to comply with \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/03053_08#section91.2\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Section 91.2 (2)\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt; of the \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/03053_00\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Environmental name=\u0026quot;update\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;Update to Minister Report\u0026lt;/h3\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;As of October 30, 2018, the \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;#template\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Update to Minister Report\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt; is required and must be completed by the responsible person in relation to a spill with the information outlined in \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section5\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Section 5 of the Spill Reporting Regulation\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;This report is required if any of the following 3 conditions are present:\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;As soon as possible at the\u0026amp;nbsp;request of the minister\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;At least once every 30 days after the date that the spill began until such time that the End-of-Spill Report is required\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;At any time the responsible person has reason to believe that information previously reported as part of the Initial Report was or has become inaccurate\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;hr /\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;h3 class=\u0026quot;bc_h3\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\u0026lt;a id=\u0026quot;end\u0026quot; name=\u0026quot;end\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;End of Spill Report\u0026lt;/h3\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;As of October 30, 2018, the \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;#template\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;End-of-Spill Report\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt; is required and must be completed by the responsible person in relation to a spill with the information outlined in \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section6\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Section 6 of the Spill Reporting Regulation\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;This report is required\u0026amp;nbsp;if:\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;The quantity spilled, or likely to spill, is equal to or greater than the quantity outlined in the Spill Reporting Regulation\u0026amp;nbsp;for a specific substance\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\t\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;The spill impacted, or was likely to impact, a body of water\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;If a spill meets the requirements for this report, the responsible person must complete the Update to Minister/End-of-Spill Report using the template provided on this page.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Once completed, \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;mailto:SpillReports@gov.bc.ca\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;submit the report by email\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt; stating the DGIR number with \u0026amp;lsquo;End-of-Spill Report\u0026amp;rsquo; in the subject line.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;If required, this report must be submitted within 30 days of the emergency response completion date of the spill as defined in \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;http://www.bclaws.ca/civix/document/id/complete/statreg/187_2017#section8\u0026quot; target=\u0026quot;_self\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Section 8 of the Spill Reporting Regulation\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\n\n\u0026lt;hr /\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;h3
